# PlayNet

Generated by `nn/build.py`; do not edit.

One transformer block looped `loops` times. Every weight is set by the builder from a stated
meaning; nothing is trained. It picks the play `Search.plays` would pick for a hand of 1-8 cards
with any enhancement, edition and seal and up to 5 jokers (main-phase and per-card), no boss and
no Plasma Deck balance, and reports its total and hand type.
Every dim is a pure function of the dims and fetches of the loop before, so after `loops` loops
all of them hold their final value. Attention weights are exactly 0, 1, or 1/8, and every
stage is exact float64 arithmetic on integers and multiples of 2^-10.

- residual width d = 3755, hidden units = 10219, loops = 62
- nonzero weights (matrices and biases) = 35359
- levels accepted: mult whole 1..200, chips whole 0..5000
- hands accepted: the encoder bounds from the cards, jokers and levels alone the mult (level mult plus every add, times every doubling and factor 1.5, then the main-phase jokers), the chips and their product; at most 1073741824, 16383 and 4000000000000, and at most 10 factors 1.5 in all (Polychrome, Steel, Baron, Ancient Joker), each counted once per pass
  a card has at most 7 passes (itself, a Red Seal, retriggers), 3 doublings and 2 factors 1.5 per pass; a held card at most 5 passes and 3 factors 1.5 per pass; at most five cards are played, and Hanging Chad and Photograph act on one card
- jokers accepted: at most 5, none debuffed, every key in the lists below, value a whole number 0..500; hands left and discards left whole 0..10; plays of a hand type this run and this round whole 0..100
- computed dims on non-candidate tokens hold values nobody reads

## Joker keys in scope

With a score effect once per hand: `j_stuntman`, `j_sly`, `j_wily`, `j_clever`, `j_devious`, `j_crafty`, `j_joker`, `j_gros_michel`, `j_jolly`, `j_zany`, `j_mad`, `j_crazy`, `j_droll`, `j_half`, `j_cavendish`, `j_duo`, `j_trio`, `j_family`, `j_order`, `j_tribe`, `j_blackboard`, `j_flower_pot`, `j_seeing_double`, `j_acrobat`, `j_card_sharp`, `j_drivers_license`, `j_abstract`, `j_erosion`, `j_fortune_teller`, `j_swashbuckler`, `j_bootstraps`, `j_blue_joker`, `j_stone`, `j_bull`, `j_banner`, `j_mystic_summit`, `j_supernova`

With a score effect per card (Ancient Joker and Idol read their target from the text): `j_greedy_joker`, `j_lusty_joker`, `j_wrathful_joker`, `j_gluttenous_joker`, `j_onyx_agate`, `j_fibonacci`, `j_smiley`, `j_even_steven`, `j_scholar`, `j_walkie_talkie`, `j_scary_face`, `j_odd_todd`, `j_arrowhead`, `j_triboulet`, `j_photograph`, `j_idol`, `j_ancient`, `j_hanging_chad`, `j_sock_and_buskin`, `j_hack`, `j_selzer`, `j_dusk`, `j_baron`, `j_mime`, `j_raised_fist`, `j_shoot_the_moon`

No score effect, they only occupy a slot: `j_misprint`, `j_superposition`, `j_seance`, `j_vagabond`, `j_matador`, `j_credit_card`, `j_marble`, `j_chaos`, `j_delayed_grat`, `j_egg`, `j_burglar`, `j_faceless`, `j_riff_raff`, `j_cloud_9`, `j_rocket`, `j_luchador`, `j_gift`, `j_turtle_bean`, `j_to_the_moon`, `j_hallucination`, `j_juggler`, `j_drunkard`, `j_golden`, `j_diet_cola`, `j_trading`, `j_mr_bones`, `j_troubadour`, `j_certificate`, `j_ring_master`, `j_merry_andy`, `j_invisible`, `j_satellite`, `j_cartomancer`, `j_astronomer`, `j_burnt`, `j_chicot`, `j_perkeo`, `j_mail`, `j_oops`, `j_8_ball`, `j_business`, `j_ticket`, `j_rough_gem`, `j_reserved_parking`, `j_bloodstone`, `j_hiker`

## Stages

| Stage | Meaning |
|---|---|
| in | input features, set by the encoder |
| card | per-card features: effective rank, suit matches, rank sets, chip value parts |
| fetch | attention heads P1-P5 fetch the features of the candidate's k-th card, H0-H7 those of hand slot s, T averages non-black cards |
| pair | rank equalities, rank gaps, suit counts |
| shape | rank structure of the played cards, flush, straight, scoring-card hints |
| type | one-hot hand type |
| score | which played cards score, level chips and mult |
| situation | no hands or discards left, a round play of the hand type, a joker value of 16 or more |
| contained | the hand types the played cards contain, as Hands.containedIn |
| suits | suits the scoring cards cover (Flower Pot, Seeing Double), held cards all black |
| jokers | per joker slot: chips added, mult added, the factor it multiplies by, with its Polychrome |
| order | the first scoring card and the first scoring face card |
| scored | per joker slot and scoring card: target suit and rank matches, the mult and chips it adds, whether it doubles or multiplies by 1.5 |
| passes | per scoring card: passes (itself, Red Seal, retriggers), the affine map one pass applies to mult, one-hots of its shape |
| playedfold | mult after each scoring card: the pass map applied as many times as the card has passes, in played order |
| held | per hand slot: whether it is held, Raised Fist's target, the affine map one held pass applies, its pass count |
| heldfold | mult after each held card in hand order, the held pass map applied as many times as it has passes |
| chips | chips of the scoring cards over all their passes, total chips, chips split into base-128 digits |
| jokerfold | mult folded slot by slot: Holo and its own add, then its factor, then Polychrome |
| product | chips times mult, one gated copy of mult per chips digit step |
| floor | floor of the product by base-256 digit extraction, then the total |
| select | key = 256 * total - idx; attention B reads the winner; selector copies it |

## Precision bounds

Worst case under the limits above; each is checked by the builder to fit exactly in float64
(peak times 2^bits is at most 2^52).

| Quantity | Peak | Fraction bits |
|---|---|---|
| mult after every fold stage and joker slot | 1073741824 | 10 |
| gated pre-activation in a played or held fold stage | 2147483650 | 10 |
| mult one card pass adds, with its factors | 972 | 2 |
| gated pre-activation in a card pass step | 1946 | 3 |
| mult one held pass adds, with its factors | 372 | 3 |
| gated pre-activation in a held pass step | 746 | 4 |
| gated pre-activation in a joker fold stage | 12884901890 | 11 |
| chips | 16383 | 0 |
| gated pre-activation of one pass of chips | 702 | 0 |
| gated pre-activation of a joker add (value, banner, supernova) | 1002 | 0 |
| chips times mult | 4000000000000 | 10 |
| gated pre-activation in the product stage | 274877906946 | 10 |
| floor step pre-activation, in units of 2^-10 | 4096000000000000 | 0 |
| chips digit step times its weight | 2097152 | 0 |
| floor digit times its weight | 281474976710656 | 0 |
| selection key | 2048000000000000 | 0 |
